{"forms":[{"form":"isochores","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["In general, a place or position with relevant parameters matching other places, as in having the same pressure, chemistry, population etc. Even within a single discipline the parameter might vary according to convenience e.g. in physics alone it might refer to velocity, volume, gravity, and many more. cf. contour, isopleth."]},{"glosses":["A line drawn on a graph showing the variation of the pressure of a constant volume of gas as it varies with temperature."],"topics":["natural-sciences","physical-sciences","physics"]},{"glosses":["A contour showing points of equal vertical thickness of strata; equal to an isopach if strata are horizontal."],"topics":["geography","geology","natural-sciences"]},{"glosses":["A sequence of DNA that has a reasonably constant proportion of pairs of guanine and cytosine bases, and is rich in genes."],"topics":["biology","genetics","medicine","natural-sciences","sciences"]}],"upstream_line":570946,"upstream_offset":2586837478,"upstream_record_sha256":"674716eaa2c9b3540103b138a9a5035a1d034a3ad8a185d3e20ec1e8f7a05535","word":"isochore"}

{"forms":[{"form":"trumpets","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The royal herald sounded a trumpet to announce their arrival.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["A musical instrument of the brass family, generally tuned to the key of B-flat; by extension, any type of lip-vibrated aerophone, most often valveless and not chromatic."],"topics":["entertainment","lifestyle","music"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The trumpets were assigned to stand at the rear of the orchestra pit.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Someone who plays the trumpet; a trumpeter."]},{"examples":[{"text":"The large bull gave a basso trumpet as he charged the hunters.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The cry of an elephant, or any similar loud cry."]},{"glosses":["One who praises, or propagates praise, or is the instrument of propagating it."],"tags":["figuratively"]},{"glosses":["A funnel, or short flaring pipe, used as a guide or conductor, as for yarn in a knitting machine."]},{"glosses":["A kind of traffic interchange involving at least one loop ramp connecting traffic either entering or leaving the terminating expressway with the far lanes of the continuous highway."]},{"glosses":["A powerful reed stop in organs, having a trumpet-like sound."]},{"glosses":["Any of various flowering plants with trumpet-shaped flowers, for example, of the genus Collomia."]},{"glosses":["A supporter of Donald Trump, especially a fervent one."],"tags":["US","capitalized","often","slang"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈtɹʌmpɪt/"}],"synonyms":[{"sense":"musical instrument","word":"beme"},{"sense":"musical instrument","word":"cornet"},{"sense":"musical instrument","word":"flugelhorn"}],"upstream_line":13805,"upstream_offset":91162032,"upstream_record_sha256":"a681376c42b227005cb6b06491f8ff359fec44d15eba0878e9fcfdf2c10ab88c","word":"trumpet"}
{"forms":[{"form":"trumpets","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"trumpeting","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"trumpeted","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"trumpeted","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The music trumpeted from the speakers, hurting my ears.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To sound loudly, be amplified"],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Cedric made a living trumpeting for the change of passersby in the subway.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To play the trumpet."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The circus trainer cracked the whip, signaling the elephant to trumpet.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["Of an elephant, to make its cry."],"tags":["ambitransitive"]},{"glosses":["To give a loud cry like that of an elephant."],"tags":["ambitransitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Andy trumpeted Jane's secret across the school, much to her embarrassment.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To proclaim loudly; to promote enthusiastically"],"tags":["transitive"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈtɹʌmpɪt/"}],"upstream_line":13806,"upstream_offset":91194508,"upstream_record_sha256":"dd3b8590b8ca8435637e09937cf355d38c06b87d5e0b5913cb9d021ee037ecdf","word":"trumpet"}
